([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/giab-truth-set-concordance-analysis))
- **giab-truth-set-stratification**: Use when comparing variant callers (e.g., miniSNV vs Clair3) on GIAB reference samples and you need stratified precision/recall/F1 rather than a single whole-genome accuracy number. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/giab-truth-set-stratification))
- **giab-truth-set-subtraction**: Use when a variant call set must be partitioned against a reference truth set — e.g. retaining Clairvoyante calls supported by both PacBio and ONT HG001 data but absent from GIAB v3.3.2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/giab-truth-set-subtraction))
- **giab-truth-vcf-block-relationship-inference**: Use when benchmarking MethPhaser or SNV-only phasing against HG002, where a GIAB trio-phased truth VCF exists and phase-block connections must be checked for correctness before computing N50 or CMRG coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/giab-truth-vcf-block-relationship-inference))
- **giab-truthset-variant-comparison**: Use when a long-read SNV VCF must be scored against GIAB high-confidence truth sets, or when accuracy (precision/recall) must be tabulated across sub-sampled coverages for GIAB samples (NA12878, NA24385, NA24149, NA24143). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/giab-truthset-variant-comparison))
- **giab-variant-annotation-of-reference-genome**: Use when building a truth-anchored simulation benchmark: a reference (e.g. chr22/hg38) must be annotated with GiAB NA12878 variants so that SNV/indel recall and precision of downstream variant callers can be scored. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/giab-variant-annotation-of-reference-genome))
- **giab-variant-benchmarking**: Use when you have variant calls (VCF) from multiple strategies — e.g. FreeBayes/SvABA on Illumina BAMs, Long Ranger on 10x Linked-Reads, or calling directly from Supernova2 assemblies — and need to score them against GIAB truth sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/giab-variant-benchmarking))
- **giab-variant-model-training**: Use when building or reproducing a deep-learning variant caller that needs a trained model on GIAB truth variants, e.g. to genotype SNPs/indels from Illumina, PacBio, or Oxford Nanopore alignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/giab-variant-model-training))
